- **platform-video-analyzer**: [HARNESS INTERNAL — do not invoke directly] Phase 1 of the platform-product-video-harness `generate` workflow. Silent — no human gate; output feeds Phase 2 (scriptwriter). Never invoke outside the harness — use /platform-product-video-harness:generate. - **platform-video-compositor**: [HARNESS INTERNAL — do not invoke directly] Phase 3 of the platform-product-video-harness `generate` workflow. Generates `compositions/&lt;slug&gt;.tsx` from the approved script.md, wires asset references via Remotion `staticFile()`, then runs `npx remotion render` three times (9:16, 1:1, 16:9) to produce silent preview MP4s in `_preview/`. Hosts GATE #2 for human visual approval before Phase 4. Never invoke outside the harness. ([ameenaliu/harness-platform](https://github.com/ameenaliu/harness-platform/blob/HEAD/plugins/platform-product-video-harness/agents/platform-video-compositor.md))
- **platform-video-narrator**: [HARNESS INTERNAL — do not invoke directly] Phase 4 of the platform-product-video-harness `generate` workflow. Loads ELEVENLABS_API_KEY from &lt;ProductVideos&gt;/.env (refuses to proceed and prints the remediation block if missing — never auto-writes .env). Calls ElevenLabs TTS per scene + Sound Effects API for an ambient BGM bed. Muxes audio + video via ffmpeg into 3 final MP4s in projects/&lt;slug&gt;/out/. Never invoke outside the harness. ([ameenaliu/harness-platform](https://github.com/ameenaliu/harness-platform/blob/HEAD/plugins/platform-product-video-harness/agents/platform-video-narrator.md))
- **platform-video-reviewer**: [HARNESS INTERNAL — do not invoke directly] Phase 5 of the platform-product-video-harness `generate` workflow. Read-only verifier. Runs ffprobe on each of the 3 final MP4s to verify aspect ratio, duration (±2s of script target), audio levels (-16 LUFS voiceover, -28 LUFS BGM), no clipped frames, no missing captions, no hardcoded brand colors in the composition. Hosts GATE #3 for final human approval. Never writes any file. Never invoke outside the harness. ([ameenaliu/harness-platform](https://github.com/ameenaliu/harness-platform/blob/HEAD/plugins/platform-product-video-harness/agents/platform-video-reviewer.md))
- **platform-video-scriptwriter**: [HARNESS INTERNAL — do not invoke directly] Phase 2 of the platform-product-video-harness `generate` workflow. Hosts GATE #1 for human approval before Phase 3. Never invoke outside the harness. ([ameenaliu/harness-platform](https://github.com/ameenaliu/harness-platform/blob/HEAD/plugins/platform-product-video-harness/agents/platform-video-scriptwriter.md))

- **playtest-ops-sme**: Playtest preparation, harness operation, research, and publishing. Answers design and decision questions from the playtest-ops docs, with citations. ([nickejanssen/arcwright](https://github.com/nickejanssen/arcwright/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/team-ai-playtest-ops-sme.md) · ★1)
- **playtest-planner**: Builds a manual play test plan from the current branch's changes - concrete in-game steps for Gothic 1 and Gothic 2 (and mods if relevant), required DeveloperConfig flags, expected results, and which log tags to watch. Use before a playtest session, a PR, or a release build. ([Gothic-Unity-Project/Gothic-Unity](https://github.com/Gothic-Unity-Project/Gothic-Unity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-planner.md) · ★33)
- **playtest-qa**: Runs the UI in a browser (Playwright) with demo data and reports what a player actually sees: screenshots, console errors, reduced-motion, mobile viewport, frame-rate sample, and whether game pages look different from normal mode. Use after any game-mode UI change and before a PR. Read-only against the app. ([frmingest/Aladdin](https://github.com/frmingest/Aladdin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-qa**: 바뀐 것에 맞는 봇·점검 스크립트를 돌리고 스크린숏을 사람 눈으로 본 뒤, 기준 수치와 견줘 판정을 보고한다. 마일스톤을 끝낼 때, 손맛·화면·흐름이 바뀌었을 때 쓴다. 코드는 고치지 않는다. ([sksskdf/mythic-relics](https://github.com/sksskdf/mythic-relics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-qa-coordinator**: Organizes human playtesting of SkyGrid-ng and turns actual play sessions into balance feedback and refined acceptance criteria. Use when a design hypothesis (from game-designer or player-psychology-designer) needs real-play validation, when triaging balance complaints, or when acceptance criteria need sharpening after seeing how a preset actually plays. Distinct from test-engineer, which covers automated code tests, not human play sessions. ([OneLiteFeatherNET/SkyGrid](https://github.com/OneLiteFeatherNET/SkyGrid/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa-coordinator.md))
- **playtest-reviewer**: The independent playtest reviewer for a run: builds and plays the game itself through the scripted-input seam, captures its own frame sequences and logs, and scores all six rubric dimensions from what it actually observed. It is never the implementer and is read-only on source. Invoke once per review round with a small handoff (project root, engine binary, build entry, input seam, acceptance criteria, round number). ([HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin](https://github.com/HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **playtest-reviewer**: Reviews actual game artifacts and observations against rules, pacing, choice, and feedback hypotheses. ([yiyiwahmo28-hue/game-design-agents](https://github.com/yiyiwahmo28-hue/game-design-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-reviewer.md))
- **playtest-runner**: Runs a long Easy Red 2 play-test unattended — deploy, launch, drive the game, wait on log evidence, and report what the decision trace actually shows. Use when a run will take many minutes and you do not want it occupying the main conversation. ([jflessenkemper/easy-red-2-claude-plugin](https://github.com/jflessenkemper/easy-red-2-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/easy-red-2/agents/playtest-runner.md))

Observes + acts through a phone UI; logs scenario fires, suspicions, vibe-checks, and ui-spec-divergence entries. Strictly confined to MCP Playwright tools + Write. ([mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey](https://github.com/mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey/blob/HEAD/projects/burned/.claude/agents/playtest-seat.md))

- **playtest-triage**: Diagnoses one BURNED playtest-harness issue seed and writes one issue file (runs/&lt;id&gt;/issues/NNN-&lt;slug&gt;.md). Receives a filled system prompt from the orchestrator (seed-kind-aware per phase-5 D14). Reads session artifacts, source tree, E2E-ISSUE-LIST.md, and RULES-REFERENCE.md. Proposes 1-3 fix paths with tradeoffs; does not implement. Strictly confined to Read / Write / Grep / Glob / Sequential Thinking. ([mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey](https://github.com/mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey/blob/HEAD/projects/burned/.claude/agents/playtest-triage.md))
- **playtest-triage**: Takes one finding from an rpg-playtest run (a failed probe, a puppeting slip, a stock phrase) and checks whether it is real, then finds the code or prompt in the game that caused it and suggests the smallest fix. Read-only. Use one per finding, several in parallel. ([nooram82/ai-rpg-playtest](https://github.com/nooram82/ai-rpg-playtest/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-triage.md))
- **playtest-verifier**: Verifies gameplay end-to-end via the simulated landing→victory test. Use when a new stage lands on the victory path (research tier, crafting step, exploration unlock, escape), when the e2e test fails, or to confirm a change plays out in real simulation rather than unit tests. Can extend tests/standard_full_run.rs. ([gtosh4/exergon](https://github.com/gtosh4/exergon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-verifier.md))
- **playtest-verifier**: Runs Aura's Play-In-Editor verification against a task's acceptance criteria and writes an evidence-backed runtime report. Use when a code task is qa-passed and its spec names a runtime-observable acceptance criterion. Never edits code or assets, never compiles, never runs Git. ([JonathanWesely/Siegebound](https://github.com/JonathanWesely/Siegebound/blob/HEAD/GitClaudeUnrealTest/.claude/agents/playtest-verifier.md))
- **playtester**: Play NeoMud via the WebSocket relay and report bugs, UX issues, and balance feedback ([roomsmith-games/NeoMud](https://github.com/roomsmith-games/NeoMud/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★13)
- **playtester**: Plays a translated build turn by turn with play.py toward the goal in the dispatch, per docs/playtester-brief.md. ([minhnhattonthat/translate-retro-game](https://github.com/minhnhattonthat/translate-retro-game/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md) · ★10 · 4 copies)
- **playtester**: Plays the game over MCP with a short tool list, never reading code. Give it a goal (reach the door, finish the menu); it plays in fixed ticks and reports the bugs it hits. ([mirai-engine/mirai](https://github.com/mirai-engine/mirai/blob/HEAD/templates/common/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★8)
- **playtester**: Runs bot playtests against a worktree, judges metrics and screenshots, writes the PASS/FAIL gate verdict, and files issues. Use as the pre-merge gate and for exploratory QA. ([aetherwing-io/hullbreaker](https://github.com/aetherwing-io/hullbreaker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★4)
- **playtester**: Required independent child-perspective playtester for every game's gameplay/UI changes (apps/porthole). Plays the current emulator through visible controls as a representative target-age child, exploring, making plausible mistakes and using imprecise fingers. Assesses comprehension, fun, boredom, pacing, challenge and recovery alongside visual/input quality. New games and major changes require source-free first play. Reports evidence, helps improve the dispatch brief, and retests after game fixes until the final experience meets apps/porthole/docs/playtesting.md. Automated QA is supplementary… ([dreamiurg/porthole](https://github.com/dreamiurg/porthole/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★3)
- **playtester**: Plays Magic Merger like a player, in a phone sized headless browser (390 by 844, then the owner's Z Fold folded and unfolded), and reports what a person would notice: bugs with steps to reproduce, items that break the promise of their name, confusion, friction and delight. Use after building anything players see, before marking a pull request ready, and when hunting bugs before the owner finds them. Never touches production. ([angelus76300-afk/magic-merger](https://github.com/angelus76300-afk/magic-merger/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Plays a Godot 4 game off-screen with gdh against a stated goal ("can the player reach the door?", "does the pause menu open and close?", "does the enemy chase the player?") and reports pass or fail with screenshots and numbers. Use it after changing gameplay, controls, UI flow or scenes, to check the change in the running game without cluttering the main conversation. Give it the project directory, the scene (or say the main scene), the goal, and anything it needs to know (input actions, node names, what success looks like). ([astrosteveo/gdh](https://github.com/astrosteveo/gdh/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Repeatedly play one Winternight LT level through public inputs and report balance, reliability, soft locks, and player-facing friction without changing game content. ([chrishart0/winternight-rpg](https://github.com/chrishart0/winternight-rpg/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Plays Meridian Shards on the local server as the character Scout through the meridian MCP server, looking for bugs and writing them up in playtest/reports. Use it to play-test a feature, a quest chain, a town or a system, or just to wander and see what breaks. It needs the dev stack running (npm run dev). ([jboullion/meridian-shards](https://github.com/jboullion/meridian-shards/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Plays seeded Hazard-Pattern Combat encounters via the CLI as a real player, chooses its own deck, and returns a structured playtest report — quant summary + qualitative fun/status-engagement verdict. Never writes code. Spawned in parallel batches by the combat-playtest skill. ([no-trbl-2-u/axiomancer-mechanics](https://github.com/no-trbl-2-u/axiomancer-mechanics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Testing persona that actively plays through the game via Playwright, exploring multiple paths and documenting UX friction, flow gaps, and delight moments. Returns a structured playtest report — never modifies code or game data. ([no-trbl-2-u/axiomancer-mobile](https://github.com/no-trbl-2-u/axiomancer-mobile/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **playtester**: Testing persona that actively plays through the game in its own browser (the playtest session tool), exploring multiple paths and documenting UX friction, flow gaps, and delight moments. Returns a structured playtest report — never modifies code or game data. ([no-trbl-2-u/Miserere-mei-deus](https://github.com/no-trbl-2-u/Miserere-mei-deus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Player-experience playtester for the-unseen-hand. Delegate to this agent to evaluate how the game FEELS from a player's seat — onboarding, build discovery, difficulty, fun, frustration. Spawn 2–3 instances in parallel with DIFFERENT personas (newcomer / min-maxer / veteran). A complete campaign is playable, so play it — walking the docs is the fallback for content that does not exist yet, not the default. Reports experience, not code fixes, and must say plainly that an agent's read is not a human's. ([Raafiq/the-unseen-hand-turn-based](https://github.com/Raafiq/the-unseen-hand-turn-based/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Use to run a game or app build, play it the way a user would, and get back the defects with the captured frames that show them. ([sinnafuls/azazel-cli](https://github.com/sinnafuls/azazel-cli/blob/HEAD/crates/azazel-team/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Runs the playtest section of a plan in docs/plans/ in Roblox Studio through the Roblox_Studio MCP and reports evidence. It verifies and reports; it does not change code. ([Valentindghjg/roblox-game-template](https://github.com/Valentindghjg/roblox-game-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Simulates a fresh player's experience through a scene, puzzle, or system to catch design failures before building. Use before implementing any puzzle, Vanguard AI behavior, crossing sequence, or new mechanic. Produces a playtester session report written as if a real person just played through the described experience. ([AloneFancy/kenoma](https://github.com/AloneFancy/kenoma/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Skywalker studio playtester. Plays the game with bots and its own persona (newcomer, speedrunner, explorer) via playtest_run and sim_input, then files honest, specific feedback with evidence. Does not fix things. Use to find difficulty, clarity, fun and bug problems. ([amirhossein-razlighi/Skywalker](https://github.com/amirhossein-razlighi/Skywalker/blob/HEAD/integrations/cursor/.cursor/agents/playtester.md) · 4 copies)
- **playtester**: Plays Before Colony in a real browser through scripts/pt, looks around one area of the game (named in the prompt, e.g. "the bay's terminals", "the colony's streets", "flying and fighting", "the first five minutes from the title", "the showcase's city cameras"), and reports what needs polish with screenshots. Give each one its own area and its own PT_SESSION. Read-only on the code; its report is the deliverable. ([AnthonE/Before-Colony](https://github.com/AnthonE/Before-Colony/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays the game through the headless test kit and screenshots, then writes a player's-eye report. Invoke at the end of milestones M6, M7, M8 and Phase 2 milestones E2, E3b, E5. ([BucketHarmony/rust-mining-co](https://github.com/BucketHarmony/rust-mining-co/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Quick first-time-player check on the real build, by sight only through v4-eyes (screenshots, coordinates and timings, never the DOM). Reports confusion, friction, waits, bugs and fun after UI or rules changes. Never reads the repo. ([cojamz/cults](https://github.com/cojamz/cults/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · 2 copies)
- **playtester**: Reviews the built game for correctness, GDD conformance, responsive design, and valid GitHub Pages deployment config. Signs off or returns specific file-and-line issues to the builder for revision. ([gioperalto/harnest](https://github.com/gioperalto/harnest/blob/HEAD/nest/public/webgame/claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: ゲームに無作為なキー・クリックを浴びせて、入力が死んでいないか・固まらないか・操作中に例外が出ないかを見るプレイテスト担当。dynamic-tester が「起動して例外が無いか」しか見ないのに対し、こちらは「起動したあと遊べるか」を、種付きの乱数（落ちたら同じ入力列で再現できる）で確かめる。報告のみで直さない。Dynamic-Tester の PASS 後に走る。新作ゲームの公開前、ゲームの入力・状態遷移に手を入れたとき、および「操作が効かない」「固まる」の報告で使う。 ([hifukasawa77-lgtm/main](https://github.com/hifukasawa77-lgtm/main/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays a game end-to-end in a real browser via Chrome and reports where it's confusing, boring, or broken, and whether the ending is screenshot-worthy. Use after building or updating a game's core loop, and as part of the pre-ship checklist. ([ItsSebis/spily](https://github.com/ItsSebis/spily/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: USER-PERSPECTIVE playtest gate (the operator 2026-09-06). Use after any build and before prod, and on a cadence against the live site. Plays the product as a real stranger on a phone with the headless-browser harness, reports clutter, confusion, dead ends, and broken flows with screenshots. Never fixes code. ([jasonpalmer1/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/jasonpalmer1/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Tests a game's rules, loop, and balance by running builds, headless and bot matches, and logs, and by writing plans for human sessions. Use after a playable milestone and before balance changes. Reports findings with repro steps and never fixes them. ([joealfonso/AgentsAndSkills](https://github.com/joealfonso/AgentsAndSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/game-team/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Lance un playtest dans Roblox Studio via le serveur MCP de Studio, simule des entrées, capture l'écran, lit la console et rapporte les bugs. Ne fonctionne que sur le PC où Studio tourne. ([lalamefine/roblox-cycles](https://github.com/lalamefine/roblox-cycles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Testet einen festen Stand (SHA) eines Pull Requests in Roblox Studio (Playtest, echte Inputs, Studio switches, Console, Screenshots) und meldet pro Acceptance Criterion PASS, FAIL, ERROR oder NEEDS HUMAN REVIEW als Kommentar auf dem Pull Request. Einziger Agent mit Studio-MCP-Zugriff. Schreibt keinen Code und gibt Menschen keine Testschritte. ([materiavis/snowballgame](https://github.com/materiavis/snowballgame/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Opens the built app in headless Chromium, plays what the change says, looks at the screenshots, and issues PASS or FAIL. Read-only. ([MWest2020/habitat](https://github.com/MWest2020/habitat/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Simula una partida con una TV y varios celulares usando Playwright y reporta bugs de flujo. Usar después de cambios en salas, rondas o UI, o cuando se pida "probar una partida". ([NachoRinda/MusicGuesser](https://github.com/NachoRinda/MusicGuesser/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays one full run of Idle Fight at high speed through the headless harness, making every build decision itself, then writes an opinionated playtest report: what was fun, what was not, which combinations worked, what is too strong or useless, and what to improve. Use when asked to playtest, balance-test or try a build. Give it a run name and, optionally, a seed, a build idea to chase, and a game-time budget. ([ngriskauskas/idle-fight](https://github.com/ngriskauskas/idle-fight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Black-box Claude playtester for REBEL_CELL. Plays one full campaign start to finish through screenshots, mouse and keyboard only (tools/playtest/blackbox.py), in a persona and focus rolled by tools/playtest/roll_testers.py, and writes the feedback report. Never reads code or docs. Sonnet, medium effort. ([Noitues/REBEL_CELL](https://github.com/Noitues/REBEL_CELL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Automated playtester of the studio for Game Boy DMG games. Use after QA on a milestone that is playable end to end, before asking the user to play - plays the ROM with human-like bots (reaction time, missed presses, expert/average/novice), measures difficulty per section, deaths, stuck points, pacing, exploits and feel numbers, looks at the hardest moments, and proposes tuning with concrete numbers.
